Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any electricity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 881. The projective resolution has length at most 933 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_881(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 881. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^933(S, F) is trivial. QED.
